| Interface | Field | Value |
|---|---|---|
| <code>interface.6.gen-ibc-core-param-inbound-enabled</code> | notes | Mismatch: the comment at interchain/modules/core/genesis.go:16 says the core params are initialised with defaults only if not set, but interchain/modules/core/genesis.go:17 calls SetParams(DefaultParams()) on every InitGenesis and ExportGenesis (interchain/modules/core/genesis.go:25) does not export them, so an export and re-import resets inbound_enabled to true; checked by IsInboundEnabled in the client, connection and channel keepers and the core msg server; the core Params type has no proto definition and is absent from the chain genesis files. 11 in-repo callers (9 non-test, 2 test). |
| <code>interface.6.gen-ibc-core-param-outbound-enabled</code> | notes | Mismatch: the comment at interchain/modules/core/genesis.go:16 says the core params are initialised with defaults only if not set, but interchain/modules/core/genesis.go:17 calls SetParams(DefaultParams()) on every InitGenesis and ExportGenesis (interchain/modules/core/genesis.go:25) does not export them, so an export and re-import resets outbound_enabled to true; checked by IsOutboundEnabled in the client, connection and channel keepers and the core msg server; the core Params type has no proto definition and is absent from the chain genesis files. 8 in-repo callers (7 non-test, 1 test). |
| <code>interface.6.gen-ibc-param-allow-messages</code> | notes | Default nil (no message allowed; "*" allows all); node/app.go:983-987 registers only the transfer and wasm routes and no interchainaccounts module, so this surface is not reachable on the chain. 2 in-repo callers (1 non-test, 1 test). |
| <code>interface.6.gen-ibc-param-allowed-clients</code> | notes | DefaultAllowedClients = [06-solomachine, 07-tendermint]; chain genesis files match; 09-localhost is not allowed and create_localhost is false. 6 in-repo callers (4 non-test, 2 test). |
| <code>interface.6.gen-ibc-param-controller-enabled</code> | notes | DefaultControllerEnabled = true; node/app.go:983-987 registers only the transfer and wasm routes and no interchainaccounts module, so this surface is not reachable on the chain. 2 in-repo callers (1 non-test, 1 test). |
| <code>interface.6.gen-ibc-param-host-enabled</code> | notes | DefaultHostEnabled = true; node/app.go:983-987 registers only the transfer and wasm routes and no interchainaccounts module, so this surface is not reachable on the chain. 2 in-repo callers (1 non-test, 1 test). |
| <code>interface.6.gen-ibc-param-max-expected-time-per-block</code> | notes | DefaultTimePerBlock = 30s; chain genesis files set 30000000000. 6 in-repo callers (4 non-test, 2 test). |
| <code>interface.6.gen-ibc-param-receive-enabled</code> | notes | DefaultReceiveEnabled = true; chain genesis files set true. 4 in-repo callers (4 non-test, 0 test). |
| <code>interface.6.gen-ibc-param-send-enabled</code> | notes | DefaultSendEnabled = true; chain genesis files set true. 10 in-repo callers (7 non-test, 3 test). |

| Decision | Title | Value | Basis | Affects |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>decision.684</code> | Legacy precompile snapshots are replay copies; the unwired v65 snapshots stay untasked | precompiles/&lt;module&gt;/legacy/&lt;version&gt;/abi.json and their Go packages are frozen per-upgrade copies served through GetVersioned for historical heights; they are covered by the interface.6.cfg-serve-0x10xx dispatch blocks and the current-ABI blocks, not by one block per snapshot. The v65 snapshots are listed in every versions file but no setup.go serves them; they equal v640 except for package names, so the gap changes no replay result and no task regenerates or deletes them (affirms decision.611). | node/tags:105 lists v6.5; precompiles/bank/versions:15 lists v6.5 while precompiles/bank/setup.go:40 ends the dispatch at v6.4.0; scripts/bump_version/README.md:9-11 regenerates setup.go from the versions files; precompiles/bank/legacy/v65/bank.go:1 and precompiles/bank/legacy/v640/bank.go:1 differ only in the package clause and the pcommon import | <code>6.0.1</code><br><code>interface.6.cfg-serve-0x1001</code><br><code>interface.6.go-pc-ibc-getversioned</code> |
